## String References

- **11361**: The $3 nods in agreement.
- **11364**: You wish to exchange your sparks?
- **11365**: Wait but a moment. Here, follow this command--er, compendium to the letter and you should be able to learn a thing or two.
- **11366**: Ah, this almost slipped my mind. Should you receive any distinct sorts of artifacts for completing an objective, I will be happy to take them off your hands.
- **11367**: Worry not, for I am not out to rob you of your glory. Quite the opposite, in fact. I will provide you with a worthwhile reward for collecting them.
- **11368**: Thanks to your donation, I now have $1 $0 in safekeeping.
- **11369**: Ohohoho... Turning in sparks, I see.
- **11370**: Hold your hoarder hares, [hero/heroine]. I love your enthusiasm, but you're not going to learn a thing without a primer. Ahhh, here it is. Have a look-see.
- **11371**: Oh, crud, that's right. You should trade me any special artifacts you get from completing objectives.
- **11372**: "Why?" What a good question! Because I'll give you a nifty-difty reward at some point, that's why.
- **11373**: Thanks for doing what I asked. Now I have a total of $1 $0 from you.
- **11374**: You want to trrrade in sparks, do you?
- **11375**: Let me wake up from my catnap first... Alright, now rrread this.
- **11376**: Wait, wait, wait! If you happen to get any purrrticularly interesting items from completing objectives, send them my way.
- **11377**: Don't worry, I'm as honest as a cat when the meat's out of reach. I thought of taking them all for myself...
- **11378**: ...but I don't want to make fur fly with the Adventurers' Mutual Aid Network, so I'll do my job like a good little kitty.
- **11379**: Grrreat! Now I have $1 $0 from you. Thanks!
- **11380**: Hm? Oh, spark exchange... Of course.
- **11381**: I'm not completely sure, but this might help set some things straight...I guess.
- **11382**: Let me add one thing. Please find it within your heart of hearts to give me anything of particular interest you get from completing objectives.
- **11383**: I'll make it worth your while...I swear! Just keep at it and I'll be sure to surprise you with a hot, steaming helping of gratitude one day.
- **11384**: Thanks for doing your part in the battle for love and peace! With this latest addition, I'm now keeping track of $1 $0 for you.
- **11385**: "Records of Eminence is a system brought to you by the caring folks at the Adventurers' Mutual Aid Network. Set your own objectives whenever you desire, and fulfill them to receive compensation.
- **11386**: "To set these tasks, select 'Objective List' from the Quests menu, then choose ones that suit your fancy.
- **11387**: "You will be rewarded with gil and sparks of eminence, which serve the same function as points, notes, credits, and other such methods of quantifying one's greatness. As an extra incentive, adventurers will earn three times the amount of sparks for completing an objective the first time.
- **11388**: "Completed objectives will vanish from your quest list unless they are repeatable. In the event that you wish to make room for different objectives, you will need to remove them manually.
- **11389**: "As a corollary regarding rewards, you'll be blessed with special remuneration once you complete several objectives. Bask in the joy of these pleasant surprises when you can.
- **11390**: "Lastly, any and all acts of vandalism directed against your $3 are strictly prohibited.
- **11391**: "Should you be found in violation of this dictum, the heavens will part and wrath incarnate will descend to mete out justice upon you."

- **11403**: Exchange for what? (On hand: $0) [Sparks of Eminence: $1./Conquest Points: $2./Imperial Standing: $3./Allied Notes: $4./Bayld: $5./Valor Pts.: $6./Aslt. Pts. (Leujaoam): $7./Aslt. Pts. (T. Grounds): $8./Aslt. Pts. (Lebros): $9./Aslt. Pts. (Periqia): $10./Aslt. Pts. (Ilrusi): $11./Cruor: $12./Kinetic Units: $13./Obs. Fragments: $14./M. Plasm Corpuscles: $15./Ballista Points: $16. (2000 max)/Unity Accolades: $17./Escha Silt: $18./Resistance Credits: $19./Nothing for the nonce.]
- **11404**: Exchange how many points? (On hand: $0) [$1 (One)./$2 (Three)./$3 (Ten)./$4 (Thirty)./None.]
- **11406**: Beware that any amount that exceeds the maximum storage limit will unceremoniously vanish into the aether.
- **11408**: Exchange how many? (On hand: $0) [One ($1)./Three ($2)./Ten ($3)./Thirty ($4)./None.]
- **11409**: Truly exchange $0 ($2)?
- **11410**: Are you quite satisfied with your selection?
- **11411**: You wish to exchange $6, $6, $6, $6, $6, and $6 for equipment?
- **11463**: I promise the world will unfold before you if you set an "Objective" under the "Records of Eminence" section of the Quests menu.
- **11464**: Hmhmhm? Whatever could you want with me, the one-and-only Love and Peace Coalition maester?
- **11465**: Anything to say? [That title sounds fake./You creep me out./Nothing. Just walk away.]
- **11466**: What a cruel, cruel world we live in. The people cry out for love with their hearts, but cannot bear to do it with their lips! They long for peace, but only seem to find it in solitude!
- **11467**: There's nothing scary about standing in the center of Western Adoulin and professing my love for humanity. I am simply following the orders of divine provenance!
- **11468**: Is your love larger than the largest kingdom and softer than a sough?
- **11469**: What? Don't tell me you've never heard of a one-man coalition.
- **11470**: Wait a second...you're one of those adventurers from the Middle Lands.
- **11471**: Oh boy, have I got something for you... You've already accomplished your first Records of Eminence objective just by calling out to me through the depths of loneliness!
- **11472**: I beg you not to stop here. No matter how difficult it may seem, do not stray from the path of self-improvement.
- **11473**: If you won't do it for yourself, then do it for all the spoils you'll get...including my undying fraternal love for you. ...Wait but the slightest of instants.
- **11474**: This doll that just appeared out of thin air is called $6. It archives your achievements and yours alone, so let's slap your name on it.
- **11475**: Eternal Flame inscribes your name upon the doll in an adorable hand.
- **11476**: You've taken Eternal Flame's passionately burning love deep inside yourself!
- **11477**: Now that you're filled with my flame of passion, you're ready to spread love and peace all over the land!
- **11478**: In case you didn't know, you go about doing this by adding objectives via the "Objective List" under the "Records of Eminence" section of the Quests menu.
- **11479**: The war for peace is never-ending, so there should be some challenges you can repeat indefinitely! Try them all out for as long as your interest holds.
- **11480**: Once you've accrued enough eminence, come see me. I'll give you some rewards...dripping with my love!
- **11481**: Oh, yeah. About that $3...
- **11482**: ...Doing that will get it out of your hair.
- **11483**: It's a brand-spanking-new model developed with "astral farina" or something in mind. It works like the luopans geomancers use.
- **11484**: Until we meet again, brave [gent/maiden]. While you're at it, don't forget to point any peace lovers you see my way. I'm always looking for people to swell my ranks, if you know what I mean.

128: 0x0880 [0x48] [System] [11386*]:
    → ""To set these tasks, select 'Objective List' from the Quests menu, then choose ones that suit your fancy."

130: 0x0884 [0x48] [System] [11387*]:
    → ""You will be rewarded with gil and sparks of eminence, which serve the same function as points, notes, credits, and other such methods of quantifying one's greatness. As an extra incentive, adventurers will earn three times the amount of sparks for completing an objective the first time."
